Overview\n\nA complete e-commerce solution featuring product catalog management, shopping cart functionality, secure checkout flow, and an admin dashboard for inventory management.\n\n## Key Features\n\n### Customer Features\n- **Product Catalog**: Browse products with advanced filtering and search\n- **Shopping Cart**: Add/remove items with persistent cart state\n- **Secure Checkout**: Features\n- **Inventory Management**: Add, edit, and remove products\n- **Order Management**: Process and fulfill customer orders\n- **Analytics Dashboard**: Sales reports and performance metrics\n- **User Management**: Manage customer accounts and permissions\n\n## Tech Stack\n\n### Frontend\n- Next.js 14+ (App Router)\n- React Server Components\n- TypeScript\n- TailwindCSS\n- Framer Motion for animations\n- React Query for data fetching\n\n### Backend & Database\n- Next.js API Routes\n- PostgreSQL with Prisma ORM\n- Redis for caching\n- NextAuth.js for authentication\n\n### Payment & Services\n- Stripe Payment Intents\n- Stripe Webhooks\n- Cloudinary for image hosting\n- Resend for email notifications\n\n### Deployment\n- Vercel (Frontend + Serverless Functions)\n- PostgreSQL on Railway/Supabase\n- Redis on Upstash\n\n## Architecture\n\n```\nEcomApp/\n├── app/                    # Next.js 14 app directory\n│   ├── (shop)/            # Shop routes\n│   ├── (admin)/           # Admin routes\n│   ├── api/               # API routes\n│   └── layout.tsx         # Root layout\n├── components/            # Reusable components\n│   ├── ui/               # Base UI components\n│   ├── forms/            # Form components\n│   └── layouts/          # Layout components\n├── lib/                   # Utility functions\n├── hooks/                 # Custom React hooks\n├── types/                 # TypeScript types\n└── prisma/               # Database schema\n```\n\n## Database Schema\n\n### Products\n- id, name, description, price, images, category, stock, createdAt, updatedAt\n\n### Orders\n- id, userId, items, total, status, paymentStatus, createdAt\n\n### Users\n- id, email, name, role, addresses, createdAt\n\n### Categories\n- id, name, slug, description\n\n## Getting Started\n\n### Prerequisites\n- Node.js 18+\n- PostgreSQL database\n- Stripe account\n- Cloudinary account\n\n### Installation\n\n1. Environment Setup\n```bash\ncp .env.example .env.local\n```\n\nRequired environment variables:\n```env\nDATABASE_URL=\"postgresql://user:password@localhost:5432/ecommerce\"\nNEXTAUTH_SECRET=\"your-secret-key\"\nNEXTAUTH_URL=\"http://localhost:3000\"\nSTRIPE_SECRET_KEY=\"sk_test_...\"\nSTRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET=\"whsec_...\"\nNEXT_PUBLIC_STRIPE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY=\"pk_test_...\"\nCLOUDINARY_CLOUD_NAME=\"...\"\nCLOUDINARY_API_KEY=\"...\"\nCLOUDINARY_API_SECRET=\"...\"\n```\n\n4. {
  "content": "# Exam Platform\n\nA comprehensive online examination and assessment system with real-time monitoring, automated grading, and role-based access control.\n\n[![Live Demo](https://img.shields.io/badge/Live%20Demo-View%20Site-blue?style=flat-square&logo=vercel)](https://examplatformclient.vercel.app)\n[![React](https://img.shields.io/badge/React-20232A?style=flat-square&logo=react&logoColor=61DAFB)](https://reactjs.org/)\n[![Node.js](https://img.shields.io/badge/Node.js-43853D?style=flat-square&logo=node.js&logoColor=white)](https://nodejs.org/)\n[![Socket.io](https://img.shields.io/badge/Socket.io-010101?style=flat-square&logo=socket.io&logoColor=white)](https://socket.io/)\n\n## Overview\n\nAn end-to-end examination platform enabling educational institutions to conduct secure online assessments. Features include multi-role support, question bank management, real-time exam monitoring, and automated result generation.\n\n## Key Features\n\n### For Students\n- **Secure Exam Environment**: Timer-based assessments with auto-submit\n- **Real-time Monitoring**: Live proctoring capabilities\n- **Instant Results**: Automated grading for objective questions\n- **Exam History**: View past exam results and analytics\n\n### For Instructors\n- **Question Bank**: Create and manage question repositories\n- **Exam Creation**: Build exams with various question types\n- **Proctoring Dashboard**: Monitor students during exams in real-time\n- **Result Analytics**: Comprehensive performance reports\n\n### For Administrators\n- **User Management**: Manage student and instructor accounts\n- **Institution Setup**: Configure exam schedules and settings\n- **Reports**: Export results and analytics\n- **Access Control**: Role-based permissions system\n\n## Tech Stack\n\n### Frontend\n- React 18+\n- React Router DOM\n- Context API + useReducer for state management\n- TailwindCSS\n- Recharts for analytics\n\n### Backend\n- Node.js & Express.js\n- Socket.io for real-time features\n- MongoDB with Mongoose\n- JWT Authentication\n- bcrypt for password hashing\n\n### Real-time Features\n- Socket.io for live monitoring\n- Redis adapter for Socket.io scaling\n- WebRTC for video proctoring (planned)\n\n## Architecture\n\n```\nExam-Platform/\n├── client/                 # React frontend\n│   ├── src/\n│   │   ├── components/\n│   │   ├── pages/\n│   │   ├── context/       # Auth & Exam context\n│   │   ├── hooks/         # Custom hooks\n│   │   └── services/      # API & Socket services\n│   └── public/\n├── server/                # Node.js backend\n│   ├── controllers/\n│   ├── models/\n│   ├── routes/\n│   ├── middleware/\n│   ├── socket/           # Socket.io handlers\n│   └── utils/\n└── README.md\n```\n\n## Getting Started\n\n### Prerequisites\n- Node.js 16+\n- MongoDB instance\n- Redis (for production Socket.io scaling)\n\n### Installation\n\n1. Clone the repository\n```bash\ngit clone https://github.com/Abhishek-Gharat/exam-platform-client.git\ncd exam-platform-client\n```\n\n2. Install server dependencies\n```bash\ncd server\nnpm install\n```\n\n3. Install client dependencies\n```bash\ncd ../client\nnpm install\n```\n\n4. Environment Configuration\n\nServer `.env`:\n```env\nPORT=5000\nMONGODB_URI=mongodb://localhost:27017/exam-platform\nJWT_SECRET=your_jwt_secret\nJWT_EXPIRE=7d\nNODE_ENV=development\n```\n\nClient `.env`:\n```env\nREACT_APP_API_URL=http://localhost:5000\nREACT_APP_SOCKET_URL=http://localhost:5000\n```\n\n5. Seed Database\n```bash\ncd server\nnpm run seed\n```\n\n6. Run Application\n```bash\n# Terminal 1 - Server\ncd server\nnpm run dev\n\n# Terminal 2 - Client\ncd client\nnpm start\n```\n\n## API Documentation\n\n### Authentication\n- `POST /api/auth/register` - Register (Student/Instructor/Admin)\n- `POST /api/auth/login` - Login\n- `GET /api/auth/me` - Get current user\n\n### Exams\n- `GET /api/exams` - Get all exams\n- `POST /api/exams` - Create exam (Instructor/Admin)\n- `GET /api/exams/:id` - Get exam details\n- `PUT /api/exams/:id` - Update exam\n- `DELETE /api/exams/:id` - Delete exam\n\n### Questions\n- `GET /api/questions` - Get question bank\n- `POST /api/questions` - Add question\n- `PUT /api/questions/:id` - Update question\n- `DELETE /api/questions/:id` - Delete question\n\n### Attempts\n- `POST /api/attempts/start` - Start exam attempt\n- `POST /api/attempts/submit` - Submit exam\n- `GET /api/attempts/:id` - Get attempt results\n\n## Socket.io Events\n\n### Client to Server\n- `join-exam` - Join exam room\n- `submit-answer` - Submit individual answer\n- `disconnect` - Handle disconnection\n\n### Server to Client\n- `exam-started` - Exam session started\n- `time-update` - Remaining time update\n- `exam-ended` - Exam auto-submit triggered\n- `proctor-alert` - Suspicious activity alert\n\n## Security Features\n\n- JWT-based authentication with role validation\n- Exam session locking (one device at a time)\n- IP address tracking\n- Browser tab switching detection\n- Copy/paste prevention during exams\n- Full-screen mode enforcement (optional)\n\n## Database Models\n\n### User\n```javascript\n{\n  name: String,\n  email: String,\n  password: String,\n  role: Enum['student', 'instructor', 'admin'],\n  institution: String,\n  createdAt: Date\n}\n```\n\n### Exam\n```javascript\n{\n  title: String,\n  description: String,\n  duration: Number, // minutes\n  totalMarks: Number,\n  passingMarks: Number,\n  questions: [ObjectId],\n  startTime: Date,\n  endTime: Date,\n  createdBy: ObjectId,\n  status: Enum['draft', 'published', 'completed']\n}\n```\n\n### Question\n```javascript\n{\n  type: Enum['mcq', 'true-false', 'subjective'],\n  question: String,\n  options: [String],\n  correctAnswer: String/Number,\n  marks: Number,\n  category: String\n}\n```\n\n## Screenshots\n\n[Add screenshots here]\n\n## Roadmap\n\n- [ ] Video proctoring with WebRTC\n- [ ] AI-powered cheating detection\n- [ ] PDF result generation\n- [ ] Bulk student import\n- [ ] Exam scheduling with calendar\n- [ ] Mobile responsive improvements\n- [ ] Offline exam mode (PWA)\n\n## Contributing\n\nContributions are welcome. Please follow the standard fork and PR workflow.\n\n## License\n\nMIT License\n\n## Contact\n\n- LinkedIn: [Abhishek Gharat](https://linkedin.com/in/abhishek-gharat)\n- Email: [your.email@domain.com]\n\n---\n\nBuilt for educational institutions\n",

}

{
  "content": "# Repository Merge Instructions\n\n## Overview\nThis document provides step-by-step instructions for merging duplicate repositories.\n\n---\n\n## 1. Booking System Merge\n\n**Merge:** booking-backend + Booking-React-app → Booking-app\n\n### Step 1: Clone the main repository\n```bash\ngit clone https://github.com/Abhishek-Gharat/Booking-app.git\ncd Booking-app\n```\n\n### Step 2: Add other repositories as remotes\n```bash\ngit remote add backend https://github.com/Abhishek-Gharat/booking-backend.git\ngit remote add react-app https://github.com/Abhishek-Gharat/Booking-React-app.git\n```\n\n### Step 3: Fetch and merge backend\n```bash\ngit fetch backend\ngit checkout -b merge-backend\ngit subtree add --prefix=server backend/main --squash\n```\n\n### Step 4: Fetch and merge React app\n```bash\ngit fetch react-app\ngit subtree add --prefix=client-archive react-app/main --squash\n```\n\n### Step 5: Review and clean up\n```bash\n# Review the structure\ngit status\n\n# Commit the merge\ngit checkout main\ngit merge merge-backend\n\n# Push to GitHub\ngit push origin main\n```\n\n### Step 6: Update structure\nCreate this directory structure:\n```\nBooking-app/\n├── client/          # Main frontend (from current Booking-app)\n├── server/          # Backend (from booking-backend)\n├── README.md        # Updated comprehensive README\n└── package.json     # Root package.json with workspaces\n```\n\n### Step 7: Delete old repositories\n```bash\n# After successful merge\ngh repo delete Abhishek-Gharat/booking-backend --yes\ngh repo delete Abhishek-Gharat/Booking-React-app --yes\n```\n\n---\n\n## 2. Exam Platform Merge\n\n**Merge:** exam-platform-server → exam-platform-client\n\n### Step 1: Clone client repository\n```bash\ngit clone https://github.com/Abhishek-Gharat/exam-platform-client.git\ncd exam-platform-client\n```\n\n### Step 2: Add server as remote\n```bash\ngit remote add server https://github.com/Abhishek-Gharat/exam-platform-server.git\n```\n\n### Step 3: Merge server code\n```bash\ngit fetch server\ngit subtree add --prefix=server server/main --squash\n```\n\n### Step 4: Update package.json\nAdd to root package.json:\n```json\n{\n  \"name\": \"exam-platform\",\n  \"private\": true,\n  \"workspaces\": [\"client\", \"server\"],\n  \"scripts\": {\n    \"dev\": \"concurrently \\\"npm run dev:server\\\" \\\"npm run dev:client\\\"\",\n    \"dev:server\": \"cd server && npm run dev\",\n    \"dev:client\": \"cd client && npm start\",\n    \"build\": \"cd client && npm run build\",\n    \"start\": \"cd server && npm start\"\n  },\n  \"devDependencies\": {\n    \"concurrently\": \"^8.0.0\"\n  }\n}\n```\n\n### Step 5: Commit and push\n```bash\ngit add .\ngit commit -m \"feat: merge server into monorepo structure\"\ngit push origin main\n```\n\n### Step 6: Delete server repository\n```bash\ngh repo delete Abhishek-Gharat/exam-platform-server --yes\n```\n\n### Step 7: Rename repository (optional)\n```bash\ngh repo rename Abhishek-Gharat/exam-platform-client exam-platform\n```\n\n---\n\n## 3. E-commerce Cleanup\n\n**Action:** Keep EcomApp, delete ecommerce-app and ecommerce-frontend\n\n### Step 1: Verify EcomApp is complete\n```bash\ngit clone https://github.com/Abhishek-Gharat/EcomApp.git\ncd EcomApp\n# Verify all code is present\n```\n\n### Step 2: Delete duplicates\n```bash\ngh repo delete Abhishek-Gharat/ecommerce-app --yes\ngh repo delete Abhishek-Gharat/ecommerce-frontend --yes\n```\n\n### Step 3: Update EcomApp README\n- Add comprehensive documentation\n- Include deployment URL\n- Add screenshots\n\n---\n\n## Alternative: Manual File Copy (Simpler)\n\nIf subtree merge is too complex, do manual merge:\n\n### For Booking System:\n```bash\n# Create new folder structure\nmkdir Booking-app-merged\ncd Booking-app-merged\n\n# Copy frontend\ncp -r ../Booking-app/* client/\n\n# Copy backend\ncp -r ../booking-backend/* server/\n\n# Initialize new git repo\ngit init\ngit add .\ngit commit -m \"Initial commit: merged booking platform\"\n\n# Push to Booking-app (force push after backup)\ngit remote add origin https://github.com/Abhishek-Gharat/Booking-app.git\ngit push --force origin main\n```\n\n---\n\n## Post-Merge Checklist\n\n- [ ] All code is present in main repository\n- [ ] README is updated with new structure\n- [ ] Old repositories are deleted\n- [ ] Local links/shortcuts updated\n- [ ] Deployment URLs still work\n- [ ] Git history preserved (optional - use --squash for clean history)\n\n---\n\n## Notes\n\n1. **Backup first:** Clone all repos locally before deleting\n2. **Test locally:** Ensure merged code works before pushing\n3. **Update documentation:** Change all references to old repos\n4. **CI/CD:** Update any GitHub Actions or deployment scripts\n",

## 3. STANDARDIZED REPOSITORY STRUCTURE

### ✅ Required Structure for FEATURED PROJECTS

```
Repository-Name/
├── README.md                    # Professional, comprehensive
├── LICENSE                      # MIT or appropriate
├── .gitignore                   # Standard Node/React ignore
├── package.json                 # Dependencies
├── CONTRIBUTING.md              # Optional but professional
│
├── src/ or client/              # Source code
├── server/ or api/              # Backend (if full-stack)
├── public/ or assets/           # Static assets
│
├── docs/                        # Technical documentation ONLY
│   └── ARCHITECTURE.md          # If complex enough
│   └── API.md                   # API documentation
│
├── screenshots/                 # Actual screenshots of app
│   ├── homepage.png
│   └── features.png
│
└── tests/ or __tests__/        # Test files
```

### ✅ Required Structure for LEARNING PROJECTS

```
learning-project/
├── README.md                    # Learning objectives documented
├── PROJECTS.md or INDEX.md      # List of what was learned
├── project-1/                   # Individual mini-projects
├── project-2/
└── notes.md                     # Optional learning notes
```

---

**Before:** "in these repo i have created some small projects and add learning stuff"

**After:** "Collection of mini-projects demonstrating React, Node.js, and MongoDB integration"

## 6. MERGE STRATEGY (Without Deleting History)

### Option 1: Git Subtree Merge (Preserves History)

```bash
# Merge booking-backend into Booking-app
cd Booking-app
git remote add backend https://github.com/Abhishek-Gharat/booking-backend.git
git fetch backend
git subtree add --prefix=server backend/main

# Merge Booking-React-app into Booking-app  
git remote add react-app https://github.com/Abhishek-Gharat/Booking-React-app.git
git fetch react-app
git subtree add --prefix=client-archive react-app/main
```

### Option 2: Manual Copy (Cleaner History)

```bash
# Create new structure and copy files
mkdir Booking-app-merged
cd Booking-app-merged
mkdir client server

# Copy current Booking-app to client/
cp -r ../Booking-app/* client/

# Copy booking-backend to server/
cp -r ../booking-backend/* server/

# Initialize new repo with clean history
git init
git add .
git commit -m "feat: initial commit with merged codebase"
git remote add origin https://github.com/Abhishek-Gharat/Booking-app.git
git push --force origin main
```

### After Merge:
- [ ] Verify all code is present
- [ ] Test application works
- [ ] Update README with new structure
- [ ] Archive old repositories (don't delete yet)
- [ ] Update any deployment scripts

**What Recruiters Won't See:**
